'' : x.id); var B = String(y.id == null ? '' : y.id); return A < B ? -1 : (A > B ? 1 : 0); }); } return JSON.stringify({ a: byId(anns), c: byId(chats) }); } // 通锟斤拷锟叫憋拷指锟狡ｏ拷锟斤拷 id 锟斤拷锟津）ｏ拷锟斤拷锟斤拷锟秸筹拷/锟斤拷锟饺碉拷锟斤拷锟叫憋拷锟斤拷去锟截比斤拷 function fingerprintList(list){ function clean(x){ if(!x) return null; var o = {}; for(var k in x){ if(k !== '_cloud' && x[k] !== undefined){ o[k] = x[k]; } } return o; } return JSON.stringify((list || []).map(clean).filter(function(x){ return x; }).sort(function(x, y){ var A = String(x.id == null ? '' : x.id); var B = String(y.id == null ? '' : y.id); return A < B ? -1 : (A > B ? 1 : 0); })); } // 锟斤拷锟节硷拷录 锟斤拷锟叫伙拷 <-> Supabase 锟斤拷 function periodToRow(p, coupleId){ return { couple_id: coupleId, start: p.start, remark: JSON.stringify(p) }; } }; /* ===== 锟斤拷锟接帮拷锟斤拷 ===== */ function startHeartbeat(){ var canvas = $('heart-canvas'); if(!canvas) return; var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d'); var w, h, particles = []; function resize(){ w = canvas.width = window.innerWidth; h = canvas.height = window.innerHeight; } resize(); window.addEventListener('resize', resize); function heartX(t){ return 16 * Math.pow(Math.sin(t), 3); } function heartY(t){ return 13*Math.cos(t) - 5*Math.cos(2*t) - 2*Math.cos(3*t) - Math.cos(4*t); } for(var i=0; i<180; i++){ var t = (Math.PI*2 * i) / 180; particles.push({ tx: heartX(t), ty: -heartY(t), x: Math.random()*w, y: Math.random()*h, ox: Math.random()*w, oy: Math.random()*h, size: 1.5 + Math.random()*3, hue: 340 + Math.random()*20, phase: Math.random()*Math.PI*2, speed: 0.005 + Math.random()*0.015 }); } function draw(){ if(!$('cover-screen') || !$('cover-screen').classList.contains('active')){ requestAnimationFrame(draw); return; } ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(26,10,16,0.15)'; ctx.fillRect(0,0,w,h); var scale = Math.min(w,h) * 0.025; var cx = w/2, cy = h/2 - 20; var t = Date.now() * 0.0008; for(var i=0; i<particles.length; i++){ var p = particles[i]; p.tx = heartX((Math.PI*2*i)/180 + t); p.ty = -heartY((Math.PI*2*i)/180 + t); var targetX = cx + p.tx * scale; var targetY = cy + p.ty * scale; p.x += (targetX - p.x) * 0.04; p.y += (targetY - p.y) * 0.04; var alpha = 0.6 + 0.4 * Math.sin(t * 2 + p.phase); ctx.fillStyle = 'hsla(' + p.hue + ',80%,65%,' + alpha + ')'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.size, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.fill(); } requestAnimationFrame(draw); } draw(); } /* ===== Tab 锟叫伙拷 ===== */ var _periodInit = false; window.switchTab = function(tabName, el){ if(el.classList.contains('active')) return;
